# Booking notes — every heading suppliers wrote, with counts

What this is: we read the `booking_notes` text of every Fareharbor product
and pulled out every heading a supplier wrote, then counted how often each
one appears.

## In short

| | |
|---|---|
| Products that have booking notes | **8,244** of 11,236 (73%) |
| Of those, how many have at least one heading | **5,916** (71.8%) |
| Different headings found | **3,729** |
| Times they appear in total | **17,212** |

**`what to bring` alone appears 2,097 times** — more than the next ten
headings combined. Booking notes are mostly packing lists.

## The main thing this list shows

Suppliers write the same idea many different ways. That is the whole reason
we need this list: the AI has to know that all of these mean one column.

**"What to bring" is written at least eight ways:**

| Heading | Times |
|---|---|
| what to bring | 2097 |
| what to wear | 198 |
| bring | 139 |
| please bring | 88 |
| what should you bring | 38 |
| what to bring? | 37 |
| what do you need to bring? | 36 |
| what you need to bring | 31 |

**"What's included" is written at least seven ways:**

| Heading | Times |
|---|---|
| inclusions | 280 |
| what's included | 134 |
| we provide | 58 |
| whats included | 51 |
| what’s included | 40 |
| what we provide | 38 |
| what is included? | 37 |

## Careful — some things in the list are not really headings

| Line | Times | What it actually is |
|---|---|---|
| `please arrive 15 minutes prior` | 48 | an instruction — real content, must be kept |
| `we look forward to seeing you soon!` | 42 | a sign-off — fine to drop |
| `sydney seafood school` | 45 | the supplier's own name |
| `see you soon!` | 37 | a sign-off — fine to drop |
| `thank you for booking. we look forward to your visit.` | 32 | a greeting — fine to drop |
| `instagram competition` | 31 | one supplier's promotion |
| `abn: 36 611 842 947` | 48 | a business number |

Every heading sorted into the idea it belongs to, biggest group first.
